Let's design small-n exhaustive two-level search in detail.

Function two_level_best_for_n(n, n_starts, deadline):
- If n even, m=n//2.
- Precompute positions for each k? We'll just use combinations.
- To speed, generate bit arrays via combinations of ones positions. For each comb tuple, create h = zeros n, h[list]=1. But np.put with list each time overhead. Could create integer bitmask? For n≤20, use Python integers. But need counts. Simpler: for comb in itertools.combinations(range(n), m): b = np.zeros(n); b[comb]=1. That's 184k * 10 set =1.8M assignments, okay.
- Compute HH = np.correlate(b,b,'full') length 2n-1. For k range 0..n-1:
   index pos = n-1+k. L=n-k.
   H1 = sum b[:L] (we can precompute prefix for b each pattern) O(n) if loop. H2 = sum b[k:].
   HH_k = HH[pos]
   same = L - H1 - H2 + HH_k
   A = H1 - HH_k
   B = H2 - HH_k
   For positive coefficient: coeff = p*same + q^2*A + a^2*B
   Negative: p*same + q^2*B + a^2*A
   For k=0: L=n,H1=H2=sum=m,HH=m, same=n? same=n-m-m+m=n, A=B=0.
- To evaluate at fixed a, compute c array length 2n-1. But we can track max on fly.
- Since we need minimize over a, we can compute arrays P_same, P_heavy_plus, P_light_plus, P_heavy_minus, P_light_minus length n (k=0..n-1). Then for a grid, max. We can evaluate for a grid of maybe 21 values: A = np.arange(0,1.0001,0.05)? But optimum near 0.2, use 0 to 0.5 step0.025 (21). For each pattern, we can compute max for all a: maybe create polynomial coefficients for each dir coefficient. But loop over a inside pattern might be heavy but n patterns small? For n=20, 184k*21*20 ~75M, maybe 5s? Python loops maybe 10s. Fine. We can optimize by fixed a list length 9. Or evaluate a single a first.

Maybe we can do a deterministic search over patterns using bitwise Gray and update c arrays? But too complex. Use combinations and numpy.

However, for n=22 C=705k, maybe still okay if each pattern efficient. But time 1100, we can attempt n up to 22 or 24 if fast. But need also optimization. Let's be conservative with exhaustive n≤20. For n=20 184k, if each pattern ~20-30µs? Actually Python loop 184k with numpy operations may be 1-3 seconds? Let's estimate: Create array zeros 20, set 10, prefix sum, correlate C (small) maybe 5µs? Python overhead 184k* loop ~0.05µs? More like 0.5s? Then a grid 21*20 inner if Python loops ~75M operations, maybe 5-10s. Okay. n=22 705k* maybe 40s. Could do if deadline allows.

Coordinate descent exact continuous for n small:
We can generalize previous CD but faster and more robust. For n≤100, per variable affected length ≤n, passes maybe high. Use golden section over F(d) = max over affected coeffs (including diag approximate) with line search. To avoid nonunimodal diago, we can handle diagonal by evaluating F on a grid? Let's examine diagonal concavity. In our two-level small n, h values may be at 0.2/0.8; coordinate updates may try move to extremes; diagonal could be concave but likely not binding if target not near max of h(1-h)? At a=0.2, c0=n*0.16. For n=20 dx=0.1, C0=0.32; target0.3808. c0 not max maybe. The 1D function could still have diagonal active? If c0 max, since it's concave, the minimax over x might prefer endpoints to reduce it; golden maximizing? If max of other lines is low in middle and c0 high middle, the upper envelope has a peak in middle, minima at ends. Golden section for minimization assumes single minimum; a peak would fail. Example F=max(-x^2+1,0) on [0,1]: minima at endpoints 1, max middle; golden would converge to middle? Golden minimization on unimodal? Function with peak (non-unimodal, two minima). It might get stuck at peak? Actually standard golden for min on unimodal requires f(d1)<f(d2) implies min left; with peak it may shrink toward one side? Test f(0.38)=1.85, f(0.62)=1.62; f1>f2 -> d_lo=d1, loses left minimum? It may converge right endpoint maybe, not necessarily peak but could move to one endpoint. Could be okay? Not guaranteed.

We can avoid diagonal nonconvex by splitting coordination: treat c0 as one constraint but we can enforce h_i not near 0.5 after each pass; or use an objective that excludes k=0 and then final adjust? The true objective includes k=0. But for small n, we can include it by adding a large penalty for h_i far from 0.5: e.g., minimize max(nonzero c, diag_sum) with diag as condensate. For 1D updates, c0(x)=c0 + b x - x^2; to minimize max with others, if c0 active, optimum likely at boundary. We can solve exactly by candidate enumeration for n small. Let's implement exact 1D minimizer for max of lines plus one concave quadratic:
Given arrays base c_aff (current coefficients affected), a_coeff for change d, and diag: if has_diag, one coefficient is c0; as function of d: c_diag = c0 + d*(1 - 2h0) - d^2. For other coefficients: c_m + a_m d. We need minimize F(d) over d∈[0,1] where F = max(D(d), L(d)), L convex piecewise linear. D concave parabola. The minimal value can be found by observing that if D(d) < L(d), F=L convex; if D>L, F=D concave on interval where D above L? Max of convex and concave: The minimum occurs either at a point where L(d)=D(d) or at a minimum of L on interval where L active. Since D is concave, if F=D on an interval, its minimum on that interval is at endpoints. Thus global minimum is at one of:
- endpoints 0,1
- points where L(d) is minimized (breakpoints of upper envelope) where it is active (or any vertex)
- roots where D(d)=L_i(d) for some line.
We can find upper envelope vertices and roots. M up to n (≤700 for 700). For small n, enumerating all roots and vertices okay. For n=700, M=700 per p, O(M^2) roots too heavy. But for small n okay. For larger, we can rely on golden.

Simpler robust method for all n: Evaluate F at candidates: endpoints, h where D=0 (h=0,1 already), D vertex 0.5, intersections of D with each line (quadratic roots within [0,1]), and perhaps line-line intersections? Line-line intersections are vertices of L; minimizing L could occur at a vertex where two lines cross. But if L active at vertex, need check. Number line intersections O(M^2) too heavy. But for L convex piecewise-linear max of lines, its minimum occurs either at intersection of two lines forming upper envelope or where derivative changes from negative to positive; all such vertices can be found by computing upper hull in O(M log M). But maybe we can just use golden for L and check D roots. Since F=F(L,D), minimum either near min of L or roots D=L. We can compute min of L (max of affine) using a linear programming in 1D: minimize t s.t. t ≥ c_m + a_m d. This is LP with 2 variables; can solve by finding lower envelope of transformed functions? Equivalent to find minimal maximum of lines. n=700 O(n^2) candidate intersections maybe too heavy per p; but previous golden works. Two-level evaluation for a bit pattern:
Input b length n, m=n//2. We can compute arrays of coefficients for all a? To find best quickly:
- Compute prefix = np.cumsum(b)
- Full = np.correlate(b,b,'full')
- Ls = n - np.arange(n)  # k=0..n-1? For k=0 L=n.
- H1 = prefix[L-1] (for L index; if L=0 none? k up to n-1 L>=1; for k=n? no)
- H2 = m - prefix[k-1] if k>0 else m. But vector: prefix = [0] + cumsum; H1=k? For k in 0..n-1, start1=0,end1=L exclusive H1 = P[L]-P[0]; H2 = P[n]-P[k].
- HH = full[n-1+k]
- same = L - H1 - H2 + HH
- A = H1 - HH (H first L second positive)
- B = H2 - HH (L first H second positive)
For each k, positive coeff coefficient(same, A, B); negative (same, B, A). For k=0, A=B=0,same=n.
We need max over k of [p*same + q^2*heavy + a^2*light] for both plus diagonal? k=0 included as same=n,heavy=light=0.
For a grid, can compute using arrays shape (2n-1?) Actually positive for k=1..n-1 and negative for k=1..n-1 total 2n-2 plus diag. V_same length 2n-1, V_h, V_l. For diag same n.
Given a, coeffs = a(1-a)*V_same + (1-a)^2*V_h + a^2*V_l. Max.
We can precompute for many a values: Agrid = np.linspace(0,1,41)? But h values [a,1-a]; if a>0.5 equivalent to swapping H/L, same objective? Swapping H/L changes heavy/light? But pattern complement (with n/2 ones) yields same set of lags? If we swap H/L, A and B swap? C symmetric? Should be symmetric under h->1-h? Actually objective h(1-h_k) under complement? ∫(1-h)(h_k) = ∫h_k(1-h) = coefficient with sign reversal? max over full symmetric, so C same. So a and 1-a equivalent (with H/L swapped). We can restrict a∈[0,0.5].
Create `two_level_max_for_coeffs(Vs,Vh,Vl,agrid)`: maybe for each pattern we want best a. We can compute V_rail? Let's define polynomial coefficients per coeff: c0 = H (V_h? because (1-a)^2 constant 1) actually expand: a-a^2)*s + (1-2a+a^2)h + a^2 l = h + a(s-2h) + a^2(h+l-s). So arrays p0=h, p1=s-2h, p2=h+l-s. For vertical a grid size G, coeffs = p0 + p1[:,None]*a + p2[:,None]*a^2. Max over coeffs axis=0. We can vectorize batches: For one pattern, p arrays length M (2n-1). `np.max(p0[None,:]?` Need shape.    b = np.zeros(n); b[list(comb)] = 1.0 (comb tuple)
   P = np.empty(n+1); P[0]=0; P[1:]=np.cumsum(b)

def cd_fval_vec(d, c_aff, a, diag_idx, has_diag, unaff_max, c0, h0):
    vals = c_aff + d*a
    if has_diag:
        vals = vals.copy() ? Avoid copy: compute m_aff = max; diag_val separately.
    m_aff = vals.max()
    res = max(m_aff, unaff_max)
    if has_diag:
        diag_val = c0 + d*(1-h0-d)
        if diag_val > res: res=diag_val
    return res
```

def f_at(d, c_aff, a, diag, has_diag, unaff, c0,h0):
   if d < lo or d > hi: return inf
   m=max(c_aff + a*d)
   res=max(m, unaff)
   if has_diag: res=max(res, c0 + d*(1-h0-d))
   return res
```

Now exact CD implementation details:
```
def cd_prepare(p,n,h,c):
   m_lo = max(0, n-1-p); m_hi = min(2*n-2, n-1+p)
   ms = np.arange(m_lo, m_hi+1)
   lag = ms - (n-1)
   jf = p + lag; valid_f = (jf>=0)&(jf<n)
   jb = p - lag; valid_b = (jb>=0)&(jb<n)
   a = np.zeros(ms.size)
   if np.any(valid_f): a[valid_f] += 1.0 - h[jf[valid_f]]
   if np.any(valid_b): a[valid_b] -= h[jb[valid_b]]
   diag = np.flatnonzero(ms == n-1)[0]
   c_aff = c[ms]
   unaff = 0.0
   if m_lo>0: unaff = max(unaff, float(c[:m_lo].max()))
   if m_hi<2*n-2: unaff = max(unaff, float(c[m_hi+1:].max()))
   return ms,a,c_aff,unaff,diag
```
Could compute unaff with two max; if slice empty. M large; okay.
But if we update c incrementally, for each p `c_aff = c[ms]` view; if we later update c, fine. `a` small.

`cd_update(h,c,p,d,n,ms,a,diag)`:
```
h0=h[p]; h[p]=h0+d

Detailed function implementations:

```
def compute_c5(h, n=None):
    if n is None: n=len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')) * (2.0/n))
```

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = n/2.0
    h = np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(100):
       diff = target - h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
          room = 1-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-14: break
          h += (diff/tot)*room
          h = np.minimum(h,1)
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<1e-14: break
          h += (diff/tot)*h
          h = np.maximum(h,0)
    # final fix
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       idx=float range? choose interior
       vals=np.arange(n)+1
       idx=int(np.argmax(np.where((h>1e-9)&(h<1-1e-9), vals, 0))?) simpler idx=np.argmax(1e-9<...)
       if possible: h[idx]=min(1,max(0,h[idx]+diff)); then project once
    return h
```
Keep simple.

`smooth_obj`:
```
def smooth_obj(x,n,alpha):
   c = np.correlate(x,1-x,mode='full')
   m = c.max()
   shift = alpha*(c-m)
   mx = shift.max()
   lse = mx + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(shift-mx)))
   return (m + lse/alpha)*(2.0/n)
```
If alpha large, shift underflows okay.

`_sum_eq`, jac.

`slsqp_optimize`:
```
def slsqp_optimize(h,n,alphas,maxiter=300,deadline=None):
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_sum_eq,'jac':_sum_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    for alpha in alphas:
       if deadline is not None and time.time()>deadline-10: break
       try:
          res=minimize(smooth_obj,best_h,args=(n,alpha),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14})
          nh=project_feasible(res.x,n)
          nc=compute_c5(nh,n)
          if nc<best_c: best_h, best_c=nh, nc
       except Exception: pass
    return best_h,best_c
```
For n=700 bounds list 700 okay.

CD step grid/golden:
Need functions without lambdas.

```
def _cd_prepare(p,n,h,c):
   m_lo = max(0, n-1-p); m_hi = min(2*n-2, n-1+p)
   ms = np.arange(m_lo, m_hi+1, dtype=int)
   lag = ms - (n-1)
   jf = p + lag
   valid_f = (jf>=0)&(jf<n)
   jb = p - lag
   valid_b = (jb>=0)&(jb<n)
   a = np.zeros(ms.size, dtype=float)
   if np.any(valid_f): a[valid_f] += 1.0 - h[jf[valid_f]]
   if np.any(valid_b): a[valid_b] -= h[jb[valid_b]]
   diag = int(np.flatnonzero(ms == n-1)[0])
   c_aff = c[ms]
   unaff = 0.0
   if m_lo > 0: unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c[:m_lo])))
   if m_hi < 2*n-2: unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c[m_hi+1:])))
   return ms,a,diag,unaff
```
If unaff slice empty, np.max error; guarded.

`_cd_fval_array(d, cl, al, unaff, c0, h0)`? For vector d:
```
def _cd_f_vals(dg, cl, al, unaff, c0, h0):
    if cl.size:
       maff = np.max(cl[None,:] + dg[:,None]*al[None,:], axis=1)
    else:
       maff = np.full(dg.shape, -1e300)
    diagv = c0 + dg*(1.0 - h0 - dg)
    res = np.maximum(maff, diagv)
    if unaff > res[0]: res = np.maximum(res, unaff) # unaff scalar can broadcast
    return res
```

def _golden_f(lo, hi, cl, al, unaff, c0, h0, iters):
    gr = (np.sqrt(5)-1)/2
    a=lo; b=hi
    c1 = b - gr*(b-a); f1=_cd_f_scalar(c1,...)

    for _ in range(iters):
       if b-a<1e-14: break
       if f1 < f2:
          b = c2; c2=c1; f2=f1; c1=b-gr*(b-a); f1=...
       else:
          a=c1; c1=c2; f1=f2; c2=a+gr*(b-a); f2=...
    return (a+b)/2

Grid step:
```
def _cd_step_grid(d_lo,d_hi,cl,al,unaff,c0,h0,K,iters):
    if d_hi - d_lo < 1e-14: return 0.0, _cd_f_scalar(d_lo,...)
    if K <=2: ...
    dg = np.linspace(d_lo,d_hi,K)
    fv = _cd_f_vals(dg,cl,al,unaff,c0,h0)
    i = int(np.argmin(fv))
    best=dg[i]; fbest=fv[i]
    if iters>0:
       if i==0: lo,hi=d_lo,best
       elif i==K-1: lo,hi=best,d_hi
       else: lo,hi=dg[i-1],dg[i+1]
       dmin=_golden_f(lo,hi,...iters)
       fmin=_cd_f_scalar(dmin,...)
       if fmin < fbest: best,fbest=dmin,fmin
    return best,fbest
```

Now `cd_optimize(h,n,max_passes,deadline,exact=False,grid_K=17,golden_iters=30)`:
```
def cd_optimize(h_initial,n,max_passes,deadline,exact=False,grid_K=17,golden_iters=30):
   h = project_feasible(h_initial,n)
   c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
   best_c = float(np.max(c)*2/n)
   target = n/2
   for pass in range(max_passes):
      if time.time() > deadline: break
      h_try = h.copy(); c_try = c.copy(); improved=False
      # maybe if n>300 and grid_K small
      for p in range(n):
         if time.time() > deadline: break
         h0 = h_try[p]
         d_lo = -h0; d_hi = 1-h0
         if d_hi - d_lo < 1e-14: continue
         ms,a,diag,unaff = _cd_prepare(p,n,h_try,c_try)
         c_aff = c_try[ms]
         c0 = float(c_aff[diag])
         cl = c_aff.copy(); al = a.copy()
         cl[diag] = -1e300; al[diag]=0.0
         if exact and n <= 100:
             d_opt,f_opt = _cd_step_exact(d_lo,d_hi,cl,al,unaff,c0,h0)
         else:
             K = grid_K if n<=200 else min(grid_K,9)
             d_opt,f_opt = _cd_step_grid(d_lo,d_hi,cl,al,unaff,c0,h0,K,golden_iters)
         f0 = max(float(np.max(c_aff)) if c_aff.size else 0.0, unaff, c0) # actual current diag line? c0.
         # But c_aff includes diag line actual? At d=0, c_aff[diag]=c0. max(c_aff) okay.
         if f_opt < f0 - 1e-13:
             h_try[p] = h0 + d_opt
             c_try[ms] += a[d_opt]
             c_try[ms[diag]] -= d_opt*d_opt
             improved=True
      if improved:
         h_proj = project_feasible(h_try,n)
         c_new = np.correlate(h_proj,1-h_proj,'full')
         new_c = float(np.max(c_new)*2/n)
         if new_c < best_c - 1e-14:
             h = h_proj; c = c_new; best_c = new_c
         else:
             # If new not better, maybe discard; but could still be same with better structure? keep current.
             pass
      else:
         # if no individual improvements feasible, break? Could still after project no. break.
         # But due drift, maybe no.
         break
   return h,best_c
```

Implementation:
```
def two_level_search(n, a_vals, current_best, deadline=None):
    m=n//2
    ks=np.arange(n)
    Ls=n-ks
    idx_pos=n-1+ks
    idx_neg=n-1-ks
    pref_max = float(np.finfo(float).max)
    best_c=pref_max; best_comb=None; best_a=None
    total=math.comb(n,m)
    count=0
    for comb in itertools.combinations(range(n), m):
       count+=1
       if deadline is not None and (count & 4095)==0 and time.time()>deadline: break
       b=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int8); b[list(comb)]=1
       P=np.zeros(n+1,dtype=np.int32); np.cumsum(b, out=P[1:], dtype=np.int32) # Does np.cumsum have out? yes.
       HH=np.correlate(b,b,'full')
       H1=P[Ls]
       H2=m - P[ks]
       same = Ls - H1 - H2 + HH
       A = H1 - HH
       B = H2 - HH
       Vs = np.empty(2*n-1, dtype=float); Vh=np.empty(...); Vl=np.empty(...)
       Vs[idx_pos]=float(same); Vh[idx_pos]=float(A); Vl[idx_pos]=float(B)
       Vs[idx_neg]=float(same); Vh[idx_neg]=float(B); Vl[idx_neg]=float(A)
       # maybe free b? no
       for pi, (pa,qa,aa) in enumerate(a_pre):
           # If best_c small, maybe use max possible? skip
           coeff = pa*Vs + qa*Vh + aa*Vl
           mx = float(coeff.max())
           cval = mx * (2.0/n)
           if cval < best_c:
               best_c=cval; best_comb=comb; best_a=a_vals[pi]
    if best_comb is None: return best_c,None
    b=np.zeros(n,dtype=int); b[list(best_comb)]=1; h=best_a + (1-2*best_a)*b.astype(float)
    return best_c,h
```
`np.cumsum(b, out=P[1:], dtype=np.int32)` might fail because cumulative output dtype? Simpler `P[1:] = np.cumsum(b)`.

def random_h(n, rng, kind, ref_h=None):
    if kind == 0:
       h = rng.rand(n)
    elif kind == 1:
       m=n//2; idx=rng.choice(n,m,replace=False)
       b=np.zeros(n); b[idx]=1
       a=rng.uniform(0.0,0.35)
       h=a+(1-2*a)*b
    elif kind == 2:
       x=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False?) # samples over [0,2]; previous uses linspace(0,2,n). Use endpoint=True? Doesn't matter.
       f=rng.uniform(0.5,4.0); ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       h=0.5+0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x/n? Actually x in [0,2], use x)
    elif kind == 3:
       h=ref_h.copy()+rng.normal(0,0.02,n); clip
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```

`_cd_step_exact`:
```
def _cd_step_exact(d_lo,d_hi,cl,al,unaff,c0,h0):
    d_lo = max(d_lo,0?) Wait d interval is [d_lo,d_hi] where d_lo=-h0, d_hi=1-h0. h in [0,1]. Good.
    candidates = [d_lo,d_hi, (d_lo+d_hi)/2]
    if d_lo <=0.5 <= d_hi: candidates.append(0.5)
    # golden L
    dL = _golden_L(d_lo,d_hi,cl,al,unaff,30)
    candidates.append(dL)
    # roots
    if cl.size:
       bases=np.concatenate((cl, [unaff])); acc=np.concatenate((al,[0.0]))
    else:
       bases=np.array([unaff]); acc=np.array([0.0])
    bcoef = acc + 2*h0 -1
    ccoef = bases - c0
    disc = bcoef*bcoef - 4*ccoef
    valid = disc >= -1e-14
    if np.any(valid):
       sqrt=np.sqrt(np.maximum(disc[valid],0))
       r1=(-bcoef[valid]-sqrt)/2; r2=(-bcoef[valid]+sqrt)/2
       candidates.extend(r1.tolist()); candidates.extend(r2.tolist())
    # filter and eval
    dq=[]; for d in candidates: if d_lo-1e-12 <= d <= d_hi+1e-12: dq.append(min(max(d,d_lo),d_hi))
    if not dq: dq=[(d_lo+d_hi)/2]
    fq=_cd_f_vals(np.array(dq), cl, al, unaff, c0, h0)
    i=argmin; return dq[i],fq[i]
```
Golden L uses 30 iterations; exact candidate eval includes dL. But if L min at line vertex not exactly found, golden 30 gives high precision (interval 1e-9), okay. If D active, roots exact. Good.

   if cl.size:
      return max(float(np.max(cl + d*al)), unaff)
   return unaff
```

   f0 = max(unaff, c0)
   if clines.size: f0=max(f0, float(np.max(clines))) # clines at d=0
   if f_opt < f0 - 1e-12:
       update c_try[ms] += a[d_opt]; c_try[ms[diag]] -= d_opt*d_opt; h_try[p]=h0+d_opt; improved=True
```

Potential inefficiency: If CD improves infeasible but projection worsens every pass, we break? It will not update best, but `h` remains previous. Next pass starts from previous feasible, same; it will repeat same pass and discard. To avoid infinite identical passes, if no feasible improvement for maybe 5 passes, break. We can track `stale_passes`; if >3 break. In code, if not improved -> break already. If improved infeasible but projection not better, `stale_passed` increment; if stale>5 break. Let's implement:
```
stale=0
for pass...
   improved=False
   ...
   if improved:
      proj, new_c...
      if new_c < best_c - 1e-14: update; stale=0
      else:
         # maybe keep h_proj if close? no
         stale +=1
         if stale>=5: break

```
def combinations_masks(n,m):
   x=(1<<m)-1
   limit=1<<n
   while x<limit:
      yield x
      c = x & -x
      r = x + c
      x = (((r ^ x) >> 2)//c) | r
```

def two_level_search_bit(n, a_vals, best_c, deadline):
    m=n//2
    limit=1<<n
    x=(1<<m)-1
    lo_masks = [(1<<k) for?]
    low_masks = [(1<<L)-1 for L in range(n+1)]
    a_list = a_vals
    pa_list=[a*(1-a)...], q2, a2
    # We can also precompute min possible diagonal: skip a if 2*pa >= local_best? local_best inside.
    best_local=best_c; best_mask=None; best_ai=0
    count=0
    while x<limit:
       count+=1
       if (count & 4095)==0 and time.time()>deadline: break
       # compute per k
       # maybe initialize bests per a to large for this mask
       # To avoid looping a outer then k, do k outer and update max per a.
       # max_over_k for each a: initialize arrays? Since a_count small, use Python lists length A.
       vmax = [0.0]*A
       for k in range(n):
           L = n-k
           H1 = (x & low_masks[L]).bit_count()
           H2 = m - (x & low_masks[k]).bit_count()
           HH = (x & (x >> k)).bit_count()
           same = L - H1 - H2 + HH
           A10 = H1 - HH
           B10 = H2 - HH
           # pos: heavy A10 light B10; neg: heavy B10 light A10
           for ai in range(A):
               # pos val = pa*same + q2*A10 + a2*B10
               v = pa[ai]*same + q2[ai]*A10 + aa[ai]*B10
               if v > vmax[ai]: vmax[ai]=v
               v = pa[ai]*same + q2[ai]*B10 + aa[ai]*A10
               if v > vmax[ai]: vmax[ai]=v
       for ai,v in enumerate(vmax):
           cval = v * (2.0/n)
           if 2.0*pa[ai] < best_local? cval >? Actually vmax includes diag, so no need. if cval < best_local: update
       # next mask
```

def next_comb_mask(x):

Building h from mask:
```
def mask_to_h(mask,n,a):
    h = np.empty(n)
    for i in range(n):
       h[i] = a + (1-2*a)*((mask>>i)&1)
    return h
```
For n≤22 loop fine. Or vectorize: bits = np.array([(mask>>i)&1 for i in range(n)], dtype=float). Fine.

Bit search function:
```
def two_level_bit_search(n, a_vals, current_best, deadline):
   m=n//2; limit=1<<n; x=(1<<m)-1
   low = [0]*(n+1); for L in range(n+1): low[L]=(1<<L)-1
   A = len(a_vals); pa=[a*(1-a) ...]; q2=[(1-a)**2...]; aa=[a*a...]
   best_c = current_best if current_best<inf else float('inf')
   best_mask=None; best_ai=0
   count=0
   while x<limit:
      count +=1
      if (count & 4095)==0 and time.time()>deadline: break
      # quick lower bound diagonal? For each a, c0=2pa. If min 2pa? can't skip.
      sames=[]; Alist=[]; Blist=[]
      for k in range(n):
         L=n-k
         # low[L] = (1<<L)-1
         H1 = (x & low[L]).bit_count()
         H2 = m - (x & low[k]).bit_count()
         HH = (x & (x >> k)).bit_count()
         same = L - H1 - H2 + HH
         A10 = H1 - HH
         B10 = H2 - HH
         sames.append(same); Alist.append(A10); Blist.append(B10)
      for ai in range(A):
         pa_i=pa[ai]; q2_i=q2[ai]; aa_i=aa[ai]
         # if 2*pa_i >= best_c no need? c0=2pa_i. But vmax includes maybe; if 2*pa_i >= best_c, skip? If c0 alone >= best_c, no improvement. So skip.
         if 2.0*pa_i >= best_c - 1e-15: continue
         mx=0.0
         # local variables for speed
         S=sames; AA=Alist; BB=Blist; Lft=2.0/n
         for i in range(n):
            s=S[i]; a1=AA[i]; b1=BB[i]
            v=pa_i*s + q2_i*a1 + aa_i*b1
            if v>mx: mx=v
            v=pa_i*s + q2_i*b1 + aa_i*a1
            if v>mx: mx=v
         cval=mx*(2.0/n)
         if cval < best_c:
            best_c=cval; best_mask=x; best_ai=ai
   if best_mask is None: return best_c,None
   h=mask_to_h(best_mask,n,a_vals[best_ai]); return best_c,h
```
Because `best_c` initialized current_best. If no pattern improves current, returns None. That means we skip refine. Good. If current_best inf (no initial), best first.
But if current_best is 0.380861, and small n bit search may improve. Good.
We may miss if c equal due to tolerance; fine.

def try_round(h,n):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); c=compute...
   # binary threshold .5
   b=(h>0.5).astype(float); b=project_feasible(b,n); b=project_feasible? compute; if better c
   # top n/2
   if n>=2:
      k=int(round(n/2))
      if 0<k<n:
        idx=np.argsort(h)[-k:]
        t=np.zeros(n); t[idx]=1.0; t=project_feasible(t,n)
   # clipped
   hc=h.copy(); hc[hc<0.02]=0; hc[hc>0.98]=1; hc=project_feasible(hc,n)
   return best

Now, `random_h` kind 3 uses ref_h but if ref_h length differs, condition.
```
def random_h(n,rng,kind,ref_h):
   if kind==0:
       h=rng.rand(n)
   elif kind==1:
       m=n//2
       idx=rng.choice(n,m,replace=False)
       b=np.zeros(n); b[idx]=1.0
       a=rng.uniform(0.0,0.35)
       h=a+(1-2*a)*b
   elif kind==2:
       x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
       f=rng.uniform(0.5,4.0)
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       h=0.5+0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*(x/2)+ph) # use x coordinate? f cycles across [0,1]
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   else:
       if ref_h is not None and len(ref_h)==n:
          h=np.clip(ref_h + rng.normal(0,0.02,n),0,1)
       else:
          h=rng.rand(n)
   return project_feasible(h,n)
```
For binary kind sum exact but project.

Now, need define `compute_c5` with n param for speed:
```
def compute_c5(h, n=None):
   if n is None: n=len(h)
   return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')) * (2.0/n))
```

`slsqp_optimize` uses `compute_c5(h,n)`.

```
def build_cd_pre(n):
   pre=[]
   for p in range(n):
     m_lo = min(p, n-1-p)
     m_hi = max(n-1+p, 2*n-2-p)
     ms = np.arange(m_lo,m_hi+1,dtype=int)
     lag = ms - (n-1)
     jf = p + lag
     jb = p - lag
     vf = np.where((jf>=0)&(jf<n), jf, -1)
     vb = np.where((jb>=0)&(jb<n), jb, -1)
     diag = int(np.flatnonzero(ms == n-1)[0])
     pre.append((ms, vf, vb, diag, m_lo, m_hi))
   return pre
```

Now, let's write final code with compact lines. Need verify no lambdas. `np.any(vfm)` if vfm precomputed boolean. In pre, we can store `vfm = (jf >=0)`. But after replacing invalid with -1, vfm still. Use. Pre tuple `(ms, vf, vb, vfm, vbm, diag, m_lo, m_hi)`. Build:
```
def build_cd_pre(n):
    pre=[]
    end=2*n-2
    for p in range(n):
       m_lo = p if p < n-1-p else n-1-p
       m_hi = n-1+p
       hi2 = end-p
       if hi2 > m_hi: m_hi = hi2
       ms = np.arange(m_lo,m_hi+1,dtype=np.int32)
       lag = ms - (n-1)
       jf = p + lag
       jb = p - lag
       vfm = (jf >=0) & (jf < n)
       vbm = (jb >=0) & (jb < n)
       vf = np.where(vfm, jf, -1).astype(np.int32)
       vb = np.where(vbm, jb, -1).astype(np.int32)
       diag = int(np.searchsorted(ms, n-1)) # since ms sorted and includes
       pre.append((ms,vf,vb,vfm,vbm,diag,m_lo,m_hi))
    return pre
```
`np.searchsorted` returns index; ms int. Or `int(np.flatnonzero(ms == n-1)[0])`.
`m_lo = min(p, n-1-p)` yes.
`m_hi = max(n-1+p, end-p)`.

Check p=0,n=2: m_lo=0,m_hi=max(1,2)=2. ms 0,1,2. lag -1,0,1. jf -1,0,1; vf pos at 2? For lag1 jf=1 valid, vf[2]=1; vb lag-1 jb=1 valid, vb[0]=1; diag1 jf0 jb0. a for m0 = -h1; m1 =1-2h0; m2=1-h1. Good.

```
def _cd_f_vals(dg, cl, al, unaff, c0, h0):
    if cl.size:
       maff = np.max(cl[None, :] + dg[:, None] * al[None, :], axis=1)
    else:
       maff = np.full(dg.shape, -1e300)
    diag = c0 + dg * (1.0 - h0 - dg)
    res = np.maximum(maff, diag)
    return np.maximum(res, unaff)
```
`unaff` scalar broadcasts.

dv=c0+d*(1-h0-d)
return max(mv,dv,unaff)
```

`_golden_L`:
```
def _golden_L(lo,hi,cl,al,unaff,iters):
   if hi-lo<1e-14 or cl.size==0: return (lo+hi)/2
   gr=(np.sqrt(5)-1)/2
   a=lo; b=hi
   x1=b-gr*(b-a); f1=_cd_L(x1,...)
   x2=a+gr*(b-a); f2=_cd_L(x2,...)
   for _ in range(iters): ...
   return (a+b)/2
```
Need `_cd_L` scalar.

`_cd_step_grid`:
```
def _cd_step_grid(d_lo,d_hi,cl,al,unaff,c0,h0,K,iters):
   if d_hi-d_lo < 1e-14: return d_lo, _cd_f_scalar(d_lo,...)
   if K<2: K=2
   dg=np.linspace(d_lo,d_hi,K)
   fv=_cd_f_vals(dg,cl,al,unaff,c0,h0)
   i=int(np.argmin(fv))
   best=dg[i]; fvbest=fv[i]
   if iters>0:
      if i==0: lo,hi=d_lo,best
      elif i==K-1: lo,hi=best,d_hi
      else: lo,hi=dg[i-1],dg[i+1]
      dmin=_golden_f(lo,hi,cl,al,unaff,c0,h0,iters,False)
      fmin=_cd_f_scalar(dmin,cl,al,unaff,c0,h0)
      if fmin < fvbest:
         best, fvbest=dmin,fmin
   return best, fvbest
```
If best at endpoint and true min inside between best and next not captured due nonunimodal? Could also refine around next if i==0: interval d_lo,dg[1]? But if grid min at 0, function increasing initially; min likely 0. If another lower local later, grid would have lower at that later point. ok.

Exact step:
```
def _cd_step_exact(d_lo,d_hi,cl,al,unaff,c0,h0):
   dmid=(d_lo+d_hi)/2
   cand=[d_lo, d_hi, dmid]
   if d_lo <=0.5 <= d_hi: cand.append(0.5)
   if cl.size:
      dL=_golden_f(d_lo,d_hi,cl,al,unaff,c0,h0,30,True)
   else: dL=dmid
   cand.append(dL)
   if cl.size:
      bases=np.concatenate((cl, np.array([unaff], dtype=float)))
      av=np.concatenate((al, np.array([0.0], dtype=float)))
   else:
      bases=np.array([unaff], dtype=float); av=np.array([0.0], dtype=float)
   bcoef = av + 2*h0 - 1
   ccoef = bases - c0
   disc = bcoef*bcoef - 4*ccoef
   valid = disc >= -1e-12
   if np.any(valid):
      sq=np.sqrt(np.maximum(disc[valid],0.0))
      cand.extend(((-bcoef[valid]-sq)/2.0).tolist())
      cand.extend(((-bcoef[valid]+sq)/2.0).tolist())
   dq=np.array(cand,dtype=float)
   mask=(dq>=d_lo-1e-12)&(dq<=d_hi+1e-12)
   if not np.any(mask): return dmid, _cd_f_scalar(dmid,...)
   dq=np.clip(dq[mask], d_lo,d_hi)
   # optional unique? not necessary
   fq=_cd_f_vals(dq,cl,al,unaff,c0,h0)
   i=argmin; return float(dq[i]), float(fq[i])
```
If cand large ~2M; M≤199 =>397 candidates, fine.

Now, CD `eligible` if interval length >1e-13. For h0 at boundary, interval length 1. Good.

In `cd_optimize`, when exact false use grid_K. For n>200, K=9 or 11, iters=25. For exact small, no grid.

```
def cd_optimize(h0,n,max_passes,deadline,exact=False,grid_K=17,golden_iters=30):
    h=np.array(h0,float); h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full').astype(float)
    best=float(c.max())*2/n
    pre=build_cd_pre(n)
    end=2*n-2
    stale=0
    for _ in range(max_passes):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      h_try=h.copy(); c_try=c.copy(); improved=False
      for p in range(n):
        if time.time()>deadline: break
        h0v=h_try[p]
        d_lo=-h0v; d_hi=1-h0v
        if d_hi-d_lo < 1e-14: continue
        ms,vf,vb,vfm,vbm,diag,m_lo,m_hi = pre[p]
        a=np.zeros(ms.size)
        if np.any(vfm): a[vfm] += 1.0 - h_try[vf[vfm]]
        if np.any(vbm): a[vbm] -= h_try[vb[vb]]
        c_aff=c_try[ms]
        unaff=0.0
        if m_lo>0: unaff=float(np.max(c_try[:m_lo]))
        if m_hi<end:
           v=float(np.max(c_try[m_hi+1:])); if v>unaff: unaff=v
        c0=float(c_aff[diag])
        clines=np.delete(c_aff, diag); alines=np.delete(a, diag)
        if exact:
           d_opt,f_opt=_cd_step_exact(d_lo,d_hi,clines,alines,unaff,c0,h0v)
        else:
           d_opt,f_opt=_cd_step_grid(d_lo,d_hi,clines,alines,unaff,c0,h0v,grid_K,golden_iters)
        f0=c0 if c0>unaff else unaff
        if clines.size and float(np.max(clines))>f0: f0=float(np.max(clines))
        if f_opt < f0 - 1e-12:
           h_try[p]=h0v+d_opt
           c_try[ms] += a*d_opt
           c_try[ms[diag]] -= d_opt*d_opt
           improved=True
      if improved:
        h_proj=project_feasible(h_try,n)
        c_new=np.correlate(h_proj,1-h_proj,'full')
        new_c=float(np.max(c_new))*2/n
        if new_c < best_c - 1e-13:
           h=h_proj; c=c_new.astype(float); best_c=new_c; stale=0
        else:
           stale +=1
           if stale>4: break
      else:
        break
    return h,best_c
```
Potential bug: `c` initially maybe int? astype float.
`np.max(c_try[:m_lo])` if slice empty; guarded.
When `clines` empty, `np.max(clines)` not called due size.
`np.delete` if diag==0 and c_aff size 1 -> empty. Good.

If no feasible improvement but infeasible improved, stale increments. After stale>4 break. Good.

def polish_small(h,n,deadline):
    h,c=cd_optimize(h,n,30,deadline,exact=True,grid_K=33,golden_iters=40)
    if time.time()<deadline-8:
       h,c=slsqp_optimize(h,n,[200,1000,5000],250,deadline=min(deadline-4,time.time()+60))
    if time.time()<deadline-4:
       h,c=cd_optimize(h,n,20,deadline,exact=True,grid_K=33,golden_iters=40)
    return h,c
```

```
def cd_optimize(h0, n, max_passes, deadline, exact=False, grid_K=17, golden_iters=30):
    ...
    block = n if n <= 40 else max(20, min(120, n // 8))
    stale=0
    for rnd in range(max_passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       round_improved=False
       for start in range(0,n,block):
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          h_try=h.copy(); c_try=c.copy(); improved=False
          stop=min(n,start+block)
          for p in range(start,stop):
             ... update
          if improved:
             h_proj=project_feasible(h_try,n)
             c_new=np.correlate(...); newc=...
             if newc < best_c - 1e-13:
                h=h_proj; c=c_new; best_c=newc; round_improved=True; stale=0
             else:
                stale +=1
                if stale >= 8: break
       if start? if not round_improved: break
    return h,best_c
```

Let's write project_feasible:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = n / 2.0
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
            np.minimum(h, 1.0, out=h)
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
            np.maximum(h, 0.0, out=h)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12 and n:
        # adjust an interior point
        idx = int(np.argmax((h > 1e-8) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-8)))
        if not ((h[idx] > 1e-8) and (h[idx] < 1.0 - 1e-8)):
            idx = int(np.argmax(h < 1.0)) if diff > 0 else int(np.argmax(h > 0.0))
        h[idx] = float(np.clip(h[idx] + diff, 0.0, 1.0))
    return h
```
`np.argmax` on bool returns first True; if all false, 0. For diff>0, choose max room maybe; use `idx = int(np.argmax(1.0-h))` if diff>0 else `argmax(h)`. Simpler.
```
if diff>0: idx=int(np.argmax(1-h)); else idx=int(np.argmax(h))
h[idx]=clip(h[idx]+diff)
```
But adding full diff to one point may push out of [0,1] if diff large; after project diff small (≤1). If diff large and no room, issue. Use loop already. Fine.

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time, math

A_GRID = np.array([0.0, 0.04, 0.08, 0.12, 0.16, 0.20,
                   0.24, 0.28, 0.34, 0.40, 0.46])

def compute_c5(h, n=None):
    if n is None:
        n = len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * (2.0 / n))

def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = n / 2.0
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
            np.minimum(h, 1.0, out=h)
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
            np.maximum(h, 0.0, out=h)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12 and n > 0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h)) if diff > 0 else int(np.argmax(h))
        h[idx] = float(np.clip(h[idx] + diff, 0.0, 1.0))
    return h

def smooth_obj(x, n, alpha):
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    m = float(c.max())
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    mx = float(sh.max())
    lse = mx + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh - mx))))
    return (m + lse / alpha) * (2.0 / n)

def _sum_eq(x, n):
    return x.sum() - n / 2.0

def _sum_eq_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(n)

def slsqp_optimize(h, n, alphas, maxiter=300, deadline=None):
    best_h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": _sum_eq, "jac": _sum_eq_jac, "args": (n,)}]
    for alpha in alphas:
        if deadline is not None and time.time() > deadline - 8:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(smooth_obj, best_h, args=(n, alpha), method="SLSQP",
                           bounds=bounds, constraints=cons,
                           options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-14})
            nh = project_feasible(res.x, n)
            nc = compute_c5(nh, n)
            if nc < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = nh, nc
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best_h, best_c

def _cd_f_scalar(d, cl, al, unaff, c0, h0):
    mv = float(np.max(cl + d * al)) if cl.size else -1e300
    dv = c0 + d * (1.0 - h0 - d)
    return max(mv, dv, unaff)

def _cd_f_vals(dg, cl, al, unaff, c0, h0):
    if cl.size:
        maff = np.max(cl[None, :] + dg[:, None] * al[None, :], axis=1)
    else:
        maff = np.full(dg.shape, -1e300)
    dv = c0 + dg * (1.0 - h0 - dg)
    return np.maximum(np.maximum(maff, dv), unaff)

def _golden_f(lo, hi, cl, al, unaff, c0, h0, iters):
    if hi - lo < 1e-14:
        return (lo + hi) / 2.0
    gr = (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0
    a, b = float(lo), float(hi)
    x1 = b - gr * (b - a)
    x2 = a + gr * (b - a)
    f1 = _cd_f_scalar(x1, cl, al, unaff, c0, h0)
    f2 = _cd_f_scalar(x2, cl, al, unaff, c0, h0)
    for _ in range(max(0, int(iters))):
        if b - a < 1e-14:
            break
        if f1 < f2:
            b = x2
            x2 = x1
            f2 = f1
            x1 = b - gr * (b - a)
            f1 = _cd_f_scalar(x1, cl, al, unaff, c0, h0)
        else:
            a = x1
            x1 = x2
            f1 = f2
            x2 = a + gr * (b - a)
            f2 = _cd_f_scalar(x2, cl, al, unaff, c0, h0)
    return (a + b) / 2.0

def _cd_step_grid(d_lo, d_hi, cl, al, unaff, c0, h0, K, iters):
    if d_hi - d_lo < 1e-14:
        return float(d_lo), _cd_f_scalar(float(d_lo), cl, al, unaff, c0, h0)
    K = max(3, int(K))
    dg = np.linspace(d_lo, d_hi, K)
    fv = _cd_f_vals(dg, cl, al, unaff, c0, h0)
    i = int(np.argmin(fv))
    best_d = float(dg[i])
    best_f = float(fv[i])
    if iters > 0:
        if i == 0:
            lo, hi = d_lo, best_d
        elif i == K - 1:
            lo, hi = best_d, d_hi
        else:
            lo, hi = float(dg[i - 1]), float(dg[i + 1])
        dm = _golden_f(lo, hi, cl, al, unaff, c0, h0, int(iters))
        fm = _cd_f_scalar(dm, cl, al, unaff, c0, h0)
        if fm < best_f:
            best_d, best_f = float(dm), float(fm)
    return best_d, best_f

def build_cd_pre(n):
    pre = []
    end = 2 * n - 2
    for p in range(n):
        m_lo = min(p, n - 1 - p)
        m_hi = max(n - 1 + p, end - p)
        ms = np.arange(m_lo, m_hi + 1, dtype=np.int32)
        lag = ms - (n - 1)
        jf = p + lag
        jb = p - lag
        vfm = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
        vbm = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
        vf = np.where(vfm, jf, -1).astype(np.int32)
        vb = np.where(vbm, jb, -1).astype(np.int32)
        diag = int(np.searchsorted(ms, n - 1))
        pre.append((ms, vf, vb, vfm, vbm, diag, m_lo, m_hi))
    return pre

def cd_optimize(h0, n, max_rounds, deadline, grid_K=17, golden_iters=30):
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full").astype(float)
    best_c = float(c.max()) * (2.0 / n)
    pre = build_cd_pre(n)
    end = 2 * n - 2
    block = n if n <= 40 else max(20, min(120, n // 8))
    stale = 0
    for _ in range(max(0, int(max_rounds))):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        round_improved = False
        for start in range(0, n, block):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            h_try = h.copy()
            c_try = c.copy()
            improved = False
            stop = min(n, start + block)
            for p in range(start, stop):
                if time.time() > deadline:
                    break
                h0v = float(h_try[p])
                d_lo, d_hi = -h0v, 1.0 - h0v
                if d_hi - d_lo < 1e-14:
                    continue
                ms, vf, vb, vfm, vbm, diag, m_lo, m_hi = pre[p]
                a = np.zeros(ms.size, dtype=float)
                if np.any(vfm):
                    a[vfm] += 1.0 - h_try[vf[vfm]]
                if np.any(vbm):
                    a[vbm] -= h_try[vb[vbm]]
                c_aff = c_try[ms]
                unaff = 0.0
                if m_lo > 0:
                    unaff = float(np.max(c_try[:m_lo]))
                if m_hi < end:
                    unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c_try[m_hi + 1:])))
                c0 = float(c_aff[diag])
                clines = np.delete(c_aff, diag)
                alines = np.delete(a, diag)
                d_opt, f_opt = _cd_step_grid(d_lo, d_hi, clines, alines,
                                              unaff, c0, h0v, grid_K, golden_iters)
                f0 = max(unaff, c0)
                if clines.size:
                    f0 = max(f0, float(np.max(clines)))
                if f_opt < f0 - 1e-12:
                    nv = float(np.clip(h0v + d_opt, 0.0, 1.0))
                    d_act = nv - h0v
                    if abs(d_act) > 1e-15:
                        h_try[p] = nv
                        c_try[ms] += a * d_act
                        c_try[ms[diag]] -= d_act * d_act
                        improved = True
            if improved:
                h_proj = project_feasible(h_try, n)
                c_new = np.correlate(h_proj, 1.0 - h_proj, mode="full")
                new_c = float(c_new.max()) * (2.0 / n)
                if new_c < best_c - 1e-13:
                    h = h_proj
                    c = c_new.astype(float)
                    best_c = new_c
                    round_improved = True
                    stale = 0
                else:
                    stale += 1
                    if stale >= 8:
                        break
        if not round_improved:
            break
    return h, best_c

def mask_to_h(mask, n, a):
    h = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        h[i] = a + (1.0 - 2.0 * a) * ((mask >> i) & 1)
    return h

def two_level_bit_search(n, a_vals, current_best, deadline):
    m = n // 2
    limit = 1 << n
    x = (1 << m) - 1
    low = [(1 << i) - 1 for i in range(n + 1)]
    A = len(a_vals)
    pa = [av * (1.0 - av) for av in a_vals]
    q2 = [(1.0 - av) * (1.0 - av) for av in a_vals]
    aa = [av * av for av in a_vals]
    inv = 2.0 / n
    best_local = 1e100
    best_mask = None
    best_ai = 0
    count = 0
    while x < limit:
        count += 1
        if (count & 2047) == 0 and time.time() > deadline:
            break
        sames, a10s, b10s = [], [], []
        for k in range(n):
            L = n - k
            H1 = (x & low[L]).bit_count()
            H2 = m - (x & low[k]).bit_count()
            HH = (x & (x >> k)).bit_count()
            same = L - H1 - H2 + HH
            A10 = H1 - HH
            B10 = H2 - HH
            sames.append(same)
            a10s.append(A10)
            b10s.append(B10)
        for ai in range(A):
            if 2.0 * pa[ai] >= best_local - 1e-15:
                continue
            p1, q1, r1 = pa[ai], q2[ai], aa[ai]
            mx = 0.0
            for i in range(n):
                s = sames[i]
                au = a10s[i]
                bu = b10s[i]
                v = p1 * s + q1 * au + r1 * bu
                if v > mx:
                    mx = v
                v = p1 * s + q1 * bu + r1 * au
                if v > mx:
                    mx = v
            cv = mx * inv
            if cv < best_local:
                best_local = cv
                best_mask = x
                best_ai = ai
    if best_mask is None:
        return 1e100, None
    h = mask_to_h(best_mask, n, float(a_vals[best_ai]))
    if best_local < current_best + 0.004:
        return float(best_local), h
    return float(current_best), None

def random_h(n, rng, kind, ref_h):
    kind %= 4
    if kind == 1:
        m = n // 2
        idx = rng.choice(n, m, replace=False)
        b = np.zeros(n)
        b[idx] = 1.0
        a = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.5))
        h = a + (1.0 - 2.0 * a) * b
    elif kind == 2:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
        f = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 4.0))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(np.pi * f * x + ph)
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    elif kind == 3 and ref_h is not None and len(ref_h) == n:
        h = np.clip(ref_h + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n), 0.0, 1.0)
    else:
        h = rng.rand(n)
    return project_feasible(h, n)

def polish_small(h, n, deadline):
    h, c = cd_optimize(h, n, 28, deadline, grid_K=41, golden_iters=40)
    if time.time() < deadline - 18:
        alphas = [200, 1000, 5000] if n <= 30 else [100, 500, 2000]
        h, c = slsqp_optimize(h, n, alphas, 300, deadline=min(deadline - 5, time.time() + 70))
    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
        h, c = cd_optimize(h, n, 18, deadline, grid_K=41, golden_iters=40)
    return h, c

def polish_large(h, n, deadline):
    h, c = cd_optimize(h, n, 28, deadline, grid_K=11, golden_iters=30)
    if time.time() < deadline - 70:
        h, c = slsqp_optimize(h, n, [1000, 5000, 25000], 350, deadline=min(deadline - 20, time.time() + 300))
    if time.time() < deadline - 10:
        h, c = cd_optimize(h, n, 18, deadline, grid_K=11, golden_iters=30)
    return h, c

def try_round(h, n):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c5(h, n)
    best_h, best_c = h, c
    if n >= 4:
        b = (h > 0.5).astype(float)
        b = project_feasible(b, n)
        cb = compute_c5(b, n)
        if cb < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = b, cb
        k = max(1, n // 2)
        idx = np.argsort(h)[-k:]
        t = np.zeros(n)
        t[idx] = 1.0
        t = project_feasible(t, n)
        ct = compute_c5(t, n)
        if ct < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = t, ct
        hc = h.copy()
        hc[hc < 0.02] = 0.0
        hc[hc > 0.98] = 1.0
        hc = project_feasible(hc, n)
        cc = compute_c5(hc, n)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hc, cc
    return best_h, best_c

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    init_h = None
    init_n = 0
    try:
        init_h = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        init_n = len(init_h)
        init_h = project_feasible(init_h, init_n)
        best_h = init_h
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, init_n)
        best_n = init_n
    except Exception:
        best_n = 20
        best_h = np.full(best_n, 0.5)
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    small_ns = [8, 10, 12, 14, 16, 18, 20]
    for n in small_ns:
        if time.time() > deadline - 210:
            break
        if n >= 18 and time.time() > deadline - 320:
            break
        sub = min(deadline - 180, time.time() + (40 if n < 16 else 90))
        c, h = two_level_bit_search(n, A_GRID, best_c, sub)
        if h is not None and c < best_c + 0.004:
            h2, c2 = cd_optimize(h, n, 24, min(deadline - 150, time.time() + 80),
                                 grid_K=41, golden_iters=40)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, n

    if time.time() < deadline - 300:
        sub = min(deadline - 180, time.time() + 150)
        c, h = two_level_bit_search(22, A_GRID, best_c, sub)
        if h is not None and c < best_c + 0.004:
            h2, c2 = cd_optimize(h, 22, 24, min(deadline - 120, time.time() + 90),
                                 grid_K=41, golden_iters=40)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, 22

    if best_n <= 60 and time.time() < deadline - 130:
        best_h, best_c = polish_small(best_h, best_n, deadline)

    n_list = [10, 14, 20, 30, 48, 80, 150, 300]
    if init_n not in n_list and init_n > 0:
        n_list.append(init_n)

    for n in n_list:
        if time.time() > deadline - 90:
            break
        if n <= 20:
            rounds, nr, per = 28, 22, 9
        elif n <= 50:
            rounds, nr, per = 24, 12, 14
        elif n <= 150:
            rounds, nr, per = 20, 8, 28
        else:
            rounds, nr, per = 12, 5, 70
        K = 41 if n <= 30 else (21 if n <= 100 else (11 if n <= 300 else 9))
        it = 40 if n <= 30 else (30 if n <= 100 else 25)
        ref = best_h if n == best_n else None
        for r in range(nr):
            sub = min(deadline - 70, time.time() + per)
            if time.time() > sub:
                break
            h0 = random_h(n, rng, r, ref)
            h1, c1 = cd_optimize(h0, n, rounds, sub, grid_K=K, golden_iters=it)
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h1, c1, n
                ref = best_h

    if init_h is not None and (best_c > 0.38085 or best_n != init_n) and time.time() < deadline - 220:
        h0, c0 = polish_large(init_h, init_n, deadline)
        if c0 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h0, c0, init_n

    if best_n <= 60 and time.time() < deadline - 70:
        best_h, best_c = polish_small(best_h, best_n, deadline)
    elif time.time() < deadline - 90:
        best_h, best_c = polish_large(best_h, best_n, deadline)

    hr, cr = try_round(best_h, best_n)
    if cr < best_c:
        best_h, best_c = hr, cr

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
